      VALUES (i.[Id], i.[CreatedAt], i.[CreatedOnUtc], i.[IsDeleted], i.[Message], i.[Title], i.[UpdatedOnUtc], i.[UserId])
      OUTPUT INSERTED.[IsRead], i._Position;
      INSERT INTO [Notifications] ([Id], [CreatedAt], [CreatedOnUtc], [IsDeleted], [IsRead], [Message], [Title], [UpdatedOnUtc], [UserId])
      VALUES (@p167, @p168, @p169, @p170, @p171, @p172, @p173, @p174, @p175);
      MERGE [Notifications] USING (
      VALUES (@p176, @p177, @p178, @p179, @p180, @p181, @p182, @p183, 0),
      (@p184, @p185, @p186, @p187, @p188, @p189, @p190, @p191, 1),
      (@p192, @p193, @p194, @p195, @p196, @p197, @p198, @p199, 2)) AS i ([Id], [CreatedAt], [CreatedOnUtc], [IsDeleted], [Message], [Title], [UpdatedOnUtc], [UserId], _Position) ON 1=0
      WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
      INSERT ([Id], [CreatedAt], [CreatedOnUtc], [IsDeleted], [Message], [Title], [UpdatedOnUtc], [UserId])
      VALUES (i.[Id], i.[CreatedAt], i.[CreatedOnUtc], i.[IsDeleted], i.[Message], i.[Title], i.[UpdatedOnUtc], i.[UserId])
      OUTPUT INSERTED.[IsRead], i._Position;
